- **deep-research**: ~0 tok always-on. A multi-source web research workflow that verifies before it reports. Decomposes a question into search angles, fans out parallel searches, deduplicates by URL authority, extracts falsifiable claims from the sources it fetches, then puts every claim to a three-vote adversarial panel that must be refuted twice to be dropped — so what survives into the cited report is what three independent skeptics failed to kill. Ships as a workflow rather than a skill because the verification is a fan-out the model cannot reliably drive by hand.
